WDR-US-2011: 2010(M). &lt;span style="font-family: 'Segoe UI',sans-serif; font-size: 12pt;"&gt;Gage height and discharge for the period Apr. 11, 2019, to Oct. 1, 2019, were revised on Sept. 28, 2020, based on changes to the gage primary reference.&lt;/span&gt;

SCHOHARIE CREEK AT NORTH BLENHEIM NY, USGS site 01350180 (USGS-01350180), stream in Schoharie County, New York, draining 358 square miles. Daily means from 1970-10-01 to 2026-10-12, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
